# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Nested {
    item def Box {
        item content;
        part component;
        constraint checked { true }
    }
    part def Vehicle {
        state idle;
        perform action move;
    }
    occurrence def Life {
        event occurrence tick;
    }
    port def Socket {
        port pin;
    }
    action def Mission {
        action step;
        if true { action branch; }
        while true { action loopStep; }
        action counts;
        for count in counts { action iterated; }
        attribute total;
        assign total := 1;
        action receiver;
        send 1 to receiver;
        action target;
        terminate target;
    }
    connection def Cable {
        end part left;
        end part right;
    }
    interface def Link {
        end port supplier;
        end port consumer;
    }
    part def Assembly {
        part left;
        part right;
        connection connect left to right;
        interface connect left to right;
    }
    calc def Formula {
        calc step;
    }
    requirement def Parent {
        requirement child;
    }
    case def Inquiry {
        case child;
    }
    analysis def Study {
        analysis child;
    }
    verification def Check {
        verification child;
    }
    use case def Goal {
        use case child;
    }
    view def Scene {
        view inset;
        viewpoint satisfaction;
    }
    rendering def Picture {
        ref rendering detail;
    }
}
~~~
